The singular-value representation provides insight into the ill-conditioned nature of the restoration problem and enables the development of stable inversion techniques. The proposed SVD-based restoration algorithm is applied to three degraded images. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach in recovering image details and reducing blur artifacts under different motion models.

</details>


### [74] [Edges Before Embeddings: A Confidence-Aware Blur Gate for Vision-Language Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25838)
*Duy Tran Thanh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MagikaDocumentFromPixel，一种轻量级CPU友好图像质量门控，能在单核上7ms内将图像分类为清晰、模糊或不确定。通过46种配置、8次扫描的实证搜索确定输入分辨率是关键杠杆；引入基于经典选择性预测的置信感知路由形式化方法；提出Edge Prior Module (EPM)作为拉普拉斯幅值辅助输入通道，提升测试F1 +1.3点；最终方案MobileNetV3-Large+EPM在384x384上训练，F1=0.9803，AUC=0.9989，17 MB ONNX模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级视觉管线在模糊输入上会静默退化，浪费下游OCR、检索和VLM的计算资源，这些下游任务无法从模糊输入中恢复可用输出。需要一个轻量级、CPU友好的前置门控来过滤模糊图像，避免无效计算。

Method: (i) 46配置8扫描实证搜索确定输入分辨率为关键杠杆；(ii) 基于经典选择性预测的置信感知路由形式化；(iii) Edge Prior Module (EPM) 拉普拉斯幅值辅助输入通道；(iv) 观察到该门控是Magika内容检测、风险控制OCR和DocVLM中独立出现的重复设计模式。最终方案：MobileNetV3-Large + EPM，384x384训练，5尺度测试时增强。

Result: F1=0.9803，AUC=0.9989，17 MB ONNX模型，单CPU核7ms推理。相比固定尺度基线F1=0.9672提升+1.31点。局限：单一运动模糊分布、单种子结果、校准定性而非定量。

Conclusion: 提出了一种有效的轻量级模糊图像门控方法，但应用场景局限在文档类图像的运动模糊检测，与具身智能/机器人领域关联不大。

Abstract: Production vision pipelines silently degrade on blurry input, wasting compute on downstream OCR, retrieval, and vision-language model (VLM) calls that cannot recover a usable output. We present MagikaDocumentFromPixel, a lightweight, CPU-friendly image quality gate that classifies a single image as sharp, blurred, or uncertain in roughly 7 ms on a single CPU core. The contributions are (i) a recipe selected from a 46-configuration, 8-sweep empirical search that isolates input resolution as the dominant lever and shows architecture capacity only pays off at >= 384 px; (ii) a confidence-aware routing formalism grounded in classical selective prediction; (iii) the Edge Prior Module (EPM), a Laplacian-magnitude auxiliary input channel that gives the network direct access to the spectral evidence that classical blur heuristics rely on and that lifts test F1 by +1.3 points in a matched-env comparison; and (iv) an observation that the gate is one instance of a recurring design pattern that appears independently in Magika content-type detection, risk-controlled OCR with VLMs, and DocVLM. The final recipe MobileNetV3-Large with the EPM trained at 384x384 on paired GoPro Large frames, evaluated with 5-scale test-time augmentation reaches F1 = 0.9803 (AUC 0.9989) with a 17 MB ONNX artifact, improving over our fixed-scale baseline on the same hardware (F1 = 0.9672) by +1.31 points. We are explicit about limitations: results are on a single motion-blur distribution, numbers are from a single seed, and calibration is qualitative rather than measured.

</details>


### [75] [Graph it first! Enabling Reasoning on Long-form Egocentric Videos through Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25842)
*Agnese Taluzzi,Riccardo Santambrogio,Simone Mentasti,Chiara Plizzari,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于自我中心场景图（EgoSGs）的框架，将长视频压缩为紧凑的结构化文本表示，从而在MLLM的token预算内实现高效的长视频推理，在HD-EPIC VQA上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM处理长视频时受输入token限制严重，被迫大量降采样帧，导致时序和上下文信息严重丢失，尤其在自我中心视频（动态复杂、状态变化频繁、相机移动）中难以进行细粒度推理。

Method: 提出EgoSGs（自我中心场景图）作为时序锚定的结构化表示，将视频压缩为包含物体、属性、空间关系和交互的文本图结构，保留关键视觉与时序信息的同时大幅缩短输入长度，使MLLM能在token预算内处理整个视频序列。

Result: 在HD-EPIC VQA数据集上达到SOTA，在多个模型上均超越强视频基线方法。

Conclusion: 像EgoSGs这样的结构化、时序锚定的表示可以弥合长视频自我中心理解与当前MLLM上下文限制之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Existing multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) face significant challenges in processing long video sequences due to strict input token limitations. As a result, current video understanding approaches, especially in egocentric settings characterized by complex dynamics, frequent state changes, and moving cameras, are forced to massively subsample frames. This leads to severe loss of temporal and contextual information, constraining their ability to perform fine-grained video reasoning. In this work, we introduce a framework for egocentric video question answering (VQA) that overcomes these input constraints through Egocentric Scene Graphs (EgoSGs), i.e., temporally grounded, structured representations that capture objects, attributes, spatial relations, and interactions over time. By representing videos as compact, text-based scene graphs, our method preserves the essential visual and temporal information of the original video in a symbolic form that drastically reduces input length while maintaining semantic richness. Crucially, this enables MLLMs to reason efficiently over entire video sequences within their token budget. On HD-EPIC VQA, our method achieves state-of-the-art results, outperforming strong video-based baselines on multiple models and suggesting that structured, temporally grounded representations like EgoSGs can bridge long-form egocentric video understanding and the context limitations of today's MLLMs.

</details>


### [76] [Naturalness Predicts but Does Not Cause Transferability in Image Encodings of Real-World Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25844)
*Faruk Alpay,Baris Basaran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将一维信号编码为图像以复用预训练视觉骨干，但编码图像的视觉自然度（FID）与迁移准确率的相关性是由局部结构中介的，而非频谱自然性；即使完美恢复信号，冻结骨干的准确率仍远低于结构化基线（19.2% vs 73.0%），全微调也无法弥补（27% vs 67%），表明缺陷是结构性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一维信号→图像编码的视觉自然度（与自然照片的FID距离）与冻结视觉骨干迁移准确率之间的关系，并检验这种关系是否为因果性。

Method: 构建WorldStream语料库（299个时间序列，9类源识别任务）；使用7种编码和6种冻结骨干；通过可控干预（调整频谱指数β和相位加扰）分离频谱自然性与局部结构的影响。

Result: 跨编码FID与准确率Spearman ρ=-0.72；但β干预显示FID与准确率仅弱相关（Pearson -0.32），而相位加扰干预下两者强相关（Pearson -0.89）；全微调仅从19.2%提升至27%，远低于结构化基线73.0%。

Conclusion: FID预测准确率是因为Inception网络读取了与骨干网络相同的局部结构，而非频谱自然性；信号→图像编码方式存在根本性的结构缺陷，即使可逆编码也无法弥补。

Abstract: A common practice converts a one-dimensional signal into an image so that a vision backbone pretrained on natural photographs can be reused for recognition, yet the encoded image is rarely examined. We ask how the visual naturalness of an encoded image relates to its transfer accuracy under a frozen backbone. We build WorldStream, a corpus of 299 heterogeneous current-value series from key-free public APIs (weather, air quality, earthquakes, gold and oil, equities, crypto, foreign exchange, web activity and space weather), with a nine-way source-recognition task over 3143 temporally split windows. Across seven encodings and six frozen backbones, the Frechet distance of an encoding to natural images (FID) predicts its accuracy: Spearman $ρ=-0.72$. Two controlled interventions show this is not causal in the spectrum. Our invertible encoder has a single adjustable part, a spectral exponent $β$ (power $\propto |f|^{-β}$); varying $β$ moves the image toward or away from the natural-image manifold at fixed content. FID is lowest near the natural value $β\approx 2$, but frozen accuracy stays flat and far below the structured baselines (19.2% vs. 73.0%), and FID and accuracy are only weakly related over the sweep (Pearson $-0.32$). A second intervention, phase scrambling, holds the power spectrum exactly fixed while removing local structure; now FID and accuracy fall together (Pearson $-0.89$). The cross-encoding correlation is thus mediated by local structure, not spectral naturalness: FID predicts accuracy because Inception reads the same structure the backbones do. Full fine-tuning does not close the gap (27% vs. 67%), so the deficit is structural. The encoder is exactly invertible, recovering the signal from the 8-bit image at 72.9 dB, so the image doubles as a lossless record of the data.

</details>


### [77] [USS: Unified Spatial-Semantic Prompts for Embodied Visual Tracking with Latent Dynamics Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25880)
*Yuchen Xie,Xinyu Zhou,Kuangji Zuo,Yanshuo Lu,Fengrui Huang,Boyu Ma,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一空间-语义提示范式（USS），将具身视觉跟踪中的目标指示从纯文本扩展为文本、点、边界框和掩码的统一架构，结合潜在世界模型实现端到端跟踪，在真实机器人和仿真中取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身视觉跟踪（EVT）方法主要依赖语言描述指示目标，但杂乱场景中多个物体可能满足同一语义描述，导致目标歧义。需要更精确灵活的目标指示方式。

Method: 统一空间-语义提示框架USS：1）异质提示编码器（文本/点/框/掩码）；2）混合注意力融合提示token与视觉特征；3）解码为以自我为中心的路径点；4）潜世界模型通过自监督对齐预测未来表征增强时序鲁棒性。

Result: 真实机器人实验：空间目标提示相比纯文本在相似干扰物和长程跟踪场景中成功率更高。仿真基准：非MLLM方法SOTA，与MLLM方法竞争力相当且推理速度更快。

Conclusion: 空间-语义提示为具身视觉跟踪提供了更精确灵活的目标指示接口，统一多模态提示架构是有效的范式转换。

Abstract: Embodied Visual Tracking (EVT) requires an agent to continuously follow a specified target while actively moving through dynamic environments. However, prevailing EVT paradigms predominantly rely on language-based target indication. While language is expressive and convenient, cluttered scenes often contain multiple objects that satisfy the same semantic description, leading to ambiguous target grounding. We therefore propose a paradigm shift, reframing target indication in EVT from text-only specification to unified spatial-semantic prompting. Based on this paradigm, we introduce Unified Spatial-Semantic Prompts for Embodied Visual Tracking with Latent Dynamics Learning, USS, an end-to-end embodied tracking framework that supports text, point, bounding box, and mask prompts within a unified architecture. USS encodes heterogeneous prompts with modality-specific encoders, fuses prompt tokens with visual features through hybrid attention, and decodes compact prompt-conditioned representations into egocentric waypoints. To further improve temporal robustness, USS incorporates a latent world model that predicts future representations through self-supervised alignment. Real-robot experiments demonstrate that explicit spatial target cues yield higher success rates than text-only prompts, particularly in scenarios involving similar distractors and longer-horizon tracking where maintaining instance-level target identity is critical. In the simulation benchmark, USS also achieves state-of-the-art performance among non-MLLM-based methods and competitive results against recent MLLM-based approaches with faster inference speed. Our findings reveal that spatial-semantic prompting provides a more precise and flexible target indication interface for embodied visual tracking. Project site: https://arescheah.github.io/uss-project-page/.

</details>


### [78] [Enhancing Brain MRI Anomaly Detection and Reasoning with ROI Rethink and Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25894)
*Shangkun Li,Jie Xu,Yi Guo,Zeju Li,Yuanyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BrReMark框架，通过显式区域标记（bounding box）将假设-验证的接地机制引入脑MRI诊断，结合监督微调与强化学习，并利用域随机化病理合成增强策略提升OOD泛化性能，显著改善了定位精度并减少了幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视觉语言模型缺乏空间接地能力，输出不可审计且易在正常扫描上产生幻觉，限制了临床实用性。

Method: 1) 首先生成关于潜在异常的假设并通过显式边界框标记进行接地；2) 重新检查标记的证据以验证结论；3) 在结构化推理轨迹上进行监督微调；4) 使用复合奖励（定位精度+诊断推理）进行强化学习；5) 域随机化病理合成增强策略提升OOD泛化性。

Result: 内部基准：mAP50从0.74%提升至37.54%，Clinical F1达21.57%，诊断准确率45.26%；NOVA OOD基准：与SOTA相比假阳性减少45.7%，幻觉显著降低。

Conclusion: 显式的假设-验证接地机制是实现可信赖的开放式脑MRI诊断的实用路径，在分布内和OOD场景下均有效。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models typically generate diagnoses through single-pass inference without indicating which image regions support their conclusions. This lack of spatial grounding limits clinical utility: outputs cannot be audited, and models may hallucinate findings on normal scans. We present BrReMark (Brain Rethink via ROI Marking), a framework that introduces explicit region marking into brain MRI diagnosis. The model first generates hypotheses about potential abnormalities and grounds them through explicit bounding box marking, then verifies conclusions by re-examining the marked evidence. Training combines supervised fine-tuning on structured reasoning trajectories with reinforcement learning using a composite reward over localization accuracy and diagnostic reasoning. Furthermore, we integrate a domain randomization-based pathology synthesis augmentation strategy to improve the model's generalizability to out-of-distribution (OOD) data. On internal benchmark, BrReMark improves mAP50 from 0.74% to 37.54% compared to the base model, while achieving 21.57% Clinical F1 and 45.26% diagnostic accuracy. On NOVA OOD benchmark, it also achieves competitive overall performance with a 45.7% reduction in false positives compared to the state-of-the-art, indicating reduced hallucination on rare pathologies. These findings suggest that explicit hypothesis-verification grounding is a practical path toward trustworthy open-ended brain MRI diagnosis across both in-distribution and OOD settings.

</details>


### [79] [SurgAtlas: A Large-Scale Surgical Video-Language Dataset with 2,391 Hours of Open and Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25905)
*Filippos Bellos,Andre S. Gala-Garza,Miaowei Wang,Alyssa M. Hardin,Ahmad M. Hider,Yayuan Li,Jing Bi,Susan Liang,Chenliang Xu,Donald S. Likosky,Jason J. Corso*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SurgAtlas是迄今最大的手术视频-语言数据集，包含15,291个视频（2,391小时），覆盖18个外科专科、5,000+手术类型，首次大规模纳入开放手术视频，并提供专家验证的视觉问答对，通过自动化多层级标注流水线构建，微调Qwen3-VL-8B在多项手术基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视频数据集规模小、专科覆盖窄、缺乏开放手术数据，且缺乏标准化的开放手术视频理解基准，无法支撑大规模手术基础模型的预训练。

Method: 构建大规模YouTube手术视频语料库；设计自动化多层级标注流水线（LLM增强+分阶段VQA生成+显式接地验证）；采用两阶段captioning-then-instruction流水线微调Qwen3-VL-8B。

Result: 在阶段识别、三联检测、推理问答等多项手术基准上达到竞争性或SOTA结果。

Conclusion: SurgAtlas提供了大规模公开手术视频语料，支撑未来多模态手术AI系统的大规模预训练及下一代手术基础模型开发。

Abstract: We introduce SurgAtlas, the largest surgical video-language dataset to date, comprising 15,291 videos (2,391 hours) spanning 18 surgical specialties and over 5,000 procedure types, sourced entirely from publicly available YouTube content. SurgAtlas is also the first surgical video-language dataset to include open surgery at scale, with 6,182 open procedure videos alongside over 9,000 minimally invasive recordings, and the first to establish standardized benchmarks for open-surgery video understanding. We additionally provide an expert-validated subset with verified visual question-answer pairs across diverse open and minimally invasive procedures, serving as a clinically grounded benchmark for surgical reasoning. Compared with existing surgical video-language datasets, SurgAtlas provides one of the most diverse annotation schemas, combining segment-level captions, step- and phase-level descriptions, video-level surgical descriptions, and reasoning-oriented question-answer pairs organized within a hierarchical taxonomy. These annotations are constructed through an automated multi-tier pipeline with LLM-based enrichment and a staged VQA generation framework with explicit groundedness verification. The scale and diversity of SurgAtlas enable training surgical foundation models with broad procedural coverage: we finetune Qwen3-VL-8B through a two-stage captioning-then-instruction pipeline and achieve competitive or state-of-the-art results on multiple established surgical benchmarks, including phase recognition, triplet detection, and reasoning question answering. More broadly, SurgAtlas provides a large native public video corpus that can support future large-scale pretraining of multimodal surgical AI systems and contribute to the development of next-generation foundation models for surgery.

</details>


### [80] [OracleAnalyser: Analysing Implicit Semantics of Oracle Bone Scripts through MLLMs with Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25906)
*Zijia Song,Yelin Wang,Zhengyi Ma,Zitong Yu,Tianheng Wang,Jiahuan Zhang,Taorui Wang,Kaicheng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OracleAnalyser，一个基于后训练技术的甲骨文分析推理框架，通过多阶段微调Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct并引入稳定焦点偏好优化(SFPO)算法，在仅3B参数下超越更大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有甲骨文研究方法集中在识别任务，忽视了甲骨文分析这一同等重要的方面。

Method: 多阶段后训练微调Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct，提出Stable Focal Preference Optimization (SFPO)偏好优化算法，构建推理数据集、偏好数据集和新基准。

Result: 仅3B参数即超越更大规模模型，验证了优越的分析性能。

Conclusion: 后训练技术和偏好优化可有效提升小型VLM在专有领域分析任务上的能力。

Abstract: With the advancement of artificial intelligence, research on oracle bone scripts has entered a new era. However, existing methods and benchmarks remain largely confined to recognition tasks, overlooking the equally crucial aspect of oracle bone analysis. To address this gap, we propose OracleAnalyser, a reasoning framework for oracle bone analysis based on post-training techniques. Specifically, we fine-tune Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct through multiple post-training stages and introduce a new preference optimization algorithm, Stable Focal Preference Optimization (SFPO), tailored to the characteristics of oracle bone datasets. In addition, we release both an oracle bone reasoning dataset and an oracle bone preference dataset, and further construct a new benchmark to evaluate models' analytical capabilities for oracle bone scripts. Extensive experiments validate the superior analytical performance of OracleAnalyser, which achieves remarkable results with only 3B parameters, surpassing models with substantially larger scales.

</details>


### [81] [In-context Region-based Drag: Drag Any Region to Any Shape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25907)
*Jiacheng Sui,Tianyu Hao,Bingjie Gao,Li Niu,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ICRDrag，一种基于区域拖拽的图像编辑方法，利用上下文学习框架和两种注意力正则化（图像-掩码注意力一致性和源-目标注意力对应），并构建大规模配对区域数据集PRD，在编辑精度和视觉保真度上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于点的拖拽编辑存在固有的歧义性，而区域级拖拽编辑更为明确。本文旨在解决点级拖拽的歧义问题，提出更精准的区域级拖拽编辑方法。

Method: 基于上下文学习框架，输入源图像、源区域掩码和目标区域掩码；引入两种注意力正则化：1) 图像-掩码注意力一致性，确保目标区域在图像和掩码模态下关注相似的源区域；2) 源-目标注意力对应，确保源区域与目标区域之间的相互对应。同时构建大规模配对区域数据集PRD。

Result: 在定量指标和用户研究中均显著优于现有方法，实现了更高的编辑精度和视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 基于区域拖拽的ICRDrag方法有效解决了点级拖拽的歧义问题，性能大幅超越现有方法，代码、模型和数据集均已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown promise in drag-style editing. Previous works mainly focus on point-based drag, which is inherently ambiguous. This paper focuses on region-based drag and introduces a novel In-Context Region-based Drag (ICRDrag) method. Under the in-context learning framework, ICRDrag consumes a source image, a source region mask, and a target region mask, producing the target dragged image. Built upon the basic in-context learning model, we introduce two novel attention regularization: 1) image-mask attention consistency to ensure that a target region attends to similar source regions for image and mask modalities; 2) source-target attention correspondence to ensure the mutual correspondence between source and target regions. To facilitate region-based drag, we also construct Paired Region Dataset (PRD), a large-scale dataset with paired masks and images. Extensive experiments show that ICRDrag significantly outperforms existing methods in both quantitative metrics and user studies, achieving superior editing accuracy and visual fidelity. The dataset, code, and model are available at https://github.com/bcmi/ICRDrag-Region-Drag-Editing.

</details>


### [82] [FunPiQ: A New Benchmark for Pixel-Level Quality Assessment in Fundus Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25915)
*Pengwei Wang,José Morano,Virginia Mares,Hrvoje Bogunović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FunPiQ，首个像素级眼底图像质量评估基准，以及EFIQA-CP，一种基于解剖可见性可解释性设计的质量评估方法，利用非负正-无标签学习训练CNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有眼底图像质量评估依赖图像级标签且标准不一致，无法评估局部退化，缺乏可解释性。作者认为基于解剖可见性的像素级质量评估更任务无关且可解释。

Method: 构建FunPiQ像素级质量标注基准；提出EFIQA-CP可解释方法，基于解剖可见性生成质量伪标签，利用非负正-无标签学习训练CNN。

Result: 对比分类方法（事后解释）、异常检测方法和可解释设计方法，后者（尤其是EFIQA-CP）表现最优。

Conclusion: 像素级、基于解剖可见性的可解释FIQA方法优于传统方法。

Abstract: Color fundus photography (CFP) is the most common ophthalmic imaging modality for large-scale screening. However, it is highly susceptible to degradations, making robust fundus image quality assessment (FIQA) crucial. The criteria for what constitutes high-quality at the image level vary across clinical tasks, making FIQA dependent on expert knowledge. This motivated the development of automated methods and datasets. While existing datasets aim to standardize image-level quality, their criteria often differ. Furthermore, image-level labels preclude the quantitative evaluation of localized degradations, which is essential for trustworthy FIQA. We argue that pixel-level FIQA based on anatomical visibility represents a more task-agnostic, explainable approach. In this work, we introduce FunPiQ, the first FIQA benchmark to provide pixel-level quality annotations. In addition, we propose EFIQA-CP, an explainable-by-design (EBD) method that uses quality pseudo-labels based on anatomical visibility to train a CNN via Non-Negative Positive-Unlabeled learning. Extensive evaluations of classification methods with post-hoc explanations, anomaly detection methods, and EBD methods demonstrate the superior performance of the last and, particularly, of EFIQA-CP.

</details>


### [83] [Pulmonary Embolism Risk Stratification from CTPA and Medical Records: Vascular Graphs Are Not All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25956)
*Nathan Painchaud,Tristan Habémont,Morgane des Ligneris,Allan Serva,Pierre Croisille,Laurent Bertoletti,Thomas Lampert,Johannes F. Lutzeyer,Odyssée Merveille*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究肺栓塞风险分层中，仅使用电子病历和CTPA影像生物标志物（无需血液检测）是否可行。实验表明：心脏和临床特征是主要预测因子，血管生物标志物无提升，图神经网络在血管树上也未能超越简单基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺栓塞风险分层对临床决策至关重要，但血液检测经常缺失。作者希望验证能否仅凭病历和CTPA影像特征替代血液检测完成准确风险分层。

Method: 在353例私有数据集上，对比多种方法：将血管生物标志物加入表格模型，以及用图神经网络处理血管树的图结构表示。评估仅使用病历+CTPA生物标志物时的风险分层性能。

Result: 全局特征中，病历和心脏生物标志物是最重要预测因子；血管生物标志物不提升性能；甚至在血管图上使用GNN也无法超越基于全局特征的简单表格基线。

Conclusion: 反直觉地，血管图结构对PE风险分层可能不包含具有判别性的信息。

Abstract: Risk stratification for pulmonary embolism (PE) is critical for clinical decision-making. Stratification guidelines are based on patient medical records, parameters measured from computed tomography pulmonary angiography (CTPA), and blood tests. However, blood tests are often missing in routine practice. This work studies whether state-of-the-art models can accurately classify risk stratification from only medical records and biomarkers extracted from CTPA images. We benchmark different approaches to combine medical records and cardiac biomarkers with rich pulmonary vascular information; we add vascular biomarkers to tabular models and apply graph neural networks (GNNs) on the vascular tree's intrinsic graph representation. We use a private dataset (n=353) with uniquely complete data for PE risk stratification. Our results show that, among global features, medical records and cardiac biomarkers are the most significant predictors, while vascular biomarkers do not further improve stratification. Even more surprising, even GNNs on vascular graphs fail to outperform strong tabular baseline on global features. We consider hypotheses, on both models and data, that could explain this suboptimal performance. Our investigation suggests that, counter-intuitively, vascular graphs might hold no discriminative information for PE risk stratification. Code is available from https://github.com/creatis-myriad/GENESIS.

</details>


### [84] [A Benchmark for Heterogeneous Stereo Deblurring with Physically- and Epipolar-constrained Cross Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25962)
*Hoju Shin,Jiah Kim,Seung-Wook Kim,Seowon Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对智能手机异构双目系统的不对称模糊问题，提出了异构立体去模糊框架：构建了HSD真实数据集，设计了物理约束与极线约束的交叉注意力模块PECA，能高效实现跨视图特征融合，并在CNN、Transformer、NAFNet上均有一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有立体去模糊方法和基准假设同构立体设置，无法处理智能手机因相机模组硬件异构导致的严重非对称模糊。

Method: 提出PECA模块：在极线搜索窗口内施加光学导出的视差上界约束，实现物理有效的跨视图匹配；置信度加权注意力+残差融合，在可靠区域做跨引导去模糊，被遮挡区域退化为自去模糊。模块架构无关，可嵌入CNN、Transformer、NAFNet。

Result: 在HSD数据集上，PECA增强的各类基线模型在恢复质量与效率上均有提升。

Conclusion: PECA是一种轻量、架构无关的异构立体去模糊模块，通过物理约束提升跨视图融合的可靠性与效率。

Abstract: Modern stereo-capable smartphones enable immersive XR content capture. However, hardware heterogeneity across camera modules often causes severe asymmetric blur artifacts. Existing methods and benchmarks largely assume homogeneous stereo setups and therefore do not explicitly address such asymmetric degradation. To bridge this gap, we present a dedicated framework for heterogeneous stereo deblurring. First, we introduce the heterogeneous stereo deblurring (HSD) dataset, constructed from real smartphone stereo captures via multi-frame integration. Second, we propose physically- and epipolar-constrained cross attention (PECA), a lightweight module that restricts cross-view matching to an epipolar search window bounded by a optics-derived disparity upper bound. By enforcing physically valid disparity constraints, PECA enables efficient and reliable cross-view feature fusion. Moreover, our confidence-weighted attention with residual fusion emphasizes cross-guided deblurring when correspondences are reliable, while naturally falling back to self-deblurring in occluded or unreliable regions. PECA is architecture-agnostic and consistently improves CNN-, Transformer-, and NAFNet-based baselines. Extensive experiments on HSD show that PECA-enhanced models achieve improved restoration performance with favorable efficiency.

</details>


### [85] [Taxonomy-aware deep learning for hierarchical marine species classification in underwater imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25989)
*Dan Zimmerman,Dimitris A. Pados,George Sklivanitis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于分类学层次结构的深度学习框架，通过分类学加权损失、最小风险贝叶斯推理、多尺度特征编码和独立每秩分类头，在FathomNet 2025数据集上实现平均分类学距离1.581，接近第一名方案的1.535，且在分布偏移下泛化更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下海洋物种自动分类面临领域偏移、细粒度相似性和注释粒度不均衡三大挑战，现有方法难以有效应对，亟需一种能够利用分类学层次结构提升分类鲁棒性和准确性的方法。

Method: 分类学感知深度学习框架，包含四个核心组件：(1) 分类学加权损失，根据分类学距离调整损失权重；(2) 最小风险贝叶斯推理，在推理阶段对齐分类学层次；(3) 多尺度特征编码，捕获粗细粒度特征；(4) 独立每秩分类头，解耦不同分类等级的学习。

Result: 在FathomNet 2025数据集（79个海洋类别，7个分类学等级）上，平均分类学距离1.581，接近第一名1.535（差距<3%），且组件解耦后在分布偏移下泛化更好。

Conclusion: 与度量对齐的推理和简单解耦组件是提升泛化能力的关键，在领域偏移下优于依赖学习依赖关系的复杂方法。

Abstract: Automated classification of marine species from underwater imagery is essential for scalable ocean biodiversity monitoring and conservation policy. Existing approaches struggle with severe domain shift across collection platforms, fine-grained visual similarity between closely related species, and uneven annotation granularity, where many specimens can only be identified to genus or a coarser taxonomic rank. We present a taxonomy-aware deep learning framework that aligns both the training loss and the inference rule with the hierarchical structure of biological classification, combining a taxonomy-weighted loss, minimum-risk Bayesian inference, multi-scale feature encoding, and independent per-rank classification heads. Evaluated on the FathomNet 2025 dataset1 (79 marine classes across seven taxonomic ranks), the system achieves a mean taxonomic distance of 1.581, within 3% of the 1st-place solution (1.535), with the largest gains from metric-aligned inference and simple, decoupled components that generalize better than learned dependencies under distribution shift.

</details>


### [86] [From Sparse and Imperfect 2D Anchors to Consistent 3D Gaussian Street Scenes: Support-Aware Appearance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26007)
*Long Cao,Zhongquan Wang,Jie Li,Yuhan Chen,Kefei Qian,Xiangfei Huang,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出教师残差蒸馏方法，将编辑后的3D高斯街景外观残差烘焙到固定几何球谐系数中，实现跨视图一致的外观编辑，同时抑制噪声并保持内容完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯街景编辑中，独立编辑视图无法定义一致的3D目标，直接拟合会传播视图特定噪声，且现有管线无法同时处理不完美的稀疏锚点和标准光栅化部署。

Method: 构建教师残差结构空间进行频率分解、置信度估计和基元级提升；通过光栅化空间匹配提供直接优化信号；支持感知的高斯空间聚合正则化基元分配；置信门控的粗到细优化抑制噪声，最终将所有残差烘焙到固定几何球谐系数。

Result: 在Waymo街景、Tanks and Temples等数据集上，相比基于编辑的基线，在目标对齐、内容保留、伪影抑制和跨视图一致性上取得更好的平衡，消融实验验证了主要组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种实用的外观烘焙方法，教师和辅助训练模块在推理时可丢弃，实现了高质量、跨视图一致的3D街景外观编辑。

Abstract: Image priors can synthesize target conditions for 3D Gaussian street scenes, but independently edited views do not define a coherent 3D target. Direct fitting can propagate view-specific noise, while existing pipelines do not jointly handle imperfect sparse anchors and standard-rasterizer deployment. To address this gap, teacher-relative appearance residual distillation is introduced for appearance baking. A structured space for frequency decomposition, confidence estimation, and primitive-level lifting is formed by residuals between teacher anchors and original renders. The direct optimization signal is supplied by renderer-space matching, while primitive assignment is regularized by support-aware Gaussian-space aggregation. Supported detail is admitted and unsupported noise is suppressed through confidence-gated coarse-to-fine optimization, after which all residuals are baked into fixed-geometry spherical-harmonic coefficients. The teacher and auxiliary training modules are discarded at inference. Evaluation across Waymo street assets, Tanks and Temples scenes, and multiple target conditions shows a favorable overall balance of target alignment, content preservation, artifact suppression, and cross-view consistency over editing-based baselines. Ablations confirm the effectiveness of the main components. Code will be released at https://github.com/Cagares/Baking-for-3D-Gaussian.

</details>


### [87] [MIMFlow: Integrating Masked Image Modeling with Normalizing Flows for End-to-End Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26016)
*Yang Chen,Xiaowei Xu,Shuai Wang,Xinwen Zhang,Qiushi Guo,Tiezheng Ge,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MIMFlow，一种将掩码图像建模（MIM）与归一化流（NF）统一在端到端框架中的生成模型，通过VAE编码器从掩码图像推断语义潜变量，让NF专注于低频率语义流形建模，专用解码器处理高频合成，解决NF容量瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NFs作为生成模型严格可逆性导致模型容量浪费在低层像素细节上，难以捕捉高层语义结构；MIM在表征学习上表现出色但与生成管道的集成往往是模块化且脱节的。

Method: 采用VAE编码器从掩码图像推断语义潜变量，实现生成任务的解耦：NF建模简化的低频率语义流形，专用解码器负责高频合成。端到端联合优化潜语义、像素重建和生成流。

Result: ImageNet 256×256上，MIMFlow-L线性探针准确率71.3%，FID 2.50；仅使用128 tokens（比标准模型少50%），相比同规模NF基线提升32.8%。

Conclusion: MIMFlow通过语义-高频解耦有效解决了NF的容量瓶颈，使模型优先关注全局结构一致性而非冗余噪声，在生成质量和表征能力上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Normalizing Flows (NFs) are powerful generative models capable of exact density estimation and sampling. However, their strict invertibility often forces the model to exhaust its capacity on low-level pixel details, hindering the capture of high-level semantic structures. While Masked Image Modeling (MIM) has excelled in representation learning, its integration into generative pipelines has remained largely modular and disjointed. In this paper, we propose MIMFlow, a unified end-to-end framework that jointly optimizes latent semantics, pixel reconstruction, and generative flow. By employing a VAE encoder to infer semantic latent from masked images, MIMFlow achieves a principled decoupling of the generative task: the Normalizing Flow focuses on modeling a simplified, low-frequency semantic manifold, while a specialized decoder handles high-frequency synthesis. This design effectively resolves the inherent capacity bottleneck of NFs, allowing the model to prioritize global structural coherence over redundant noise. Empirical results on ImageNet 256$\times$256 show that MIMFlow-L reaches 71.3\% linear probing accuracy and an FID of 2.50. Despite using only 128 tokens (50\% fewer than standard models), it yields a 32.8\% performance gain over similar-scale NF baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/MCG-NJU/MIMFlow.

</details>


### [88] [TriViewBench: Controlled Complexity Scaling for Multi-View Structural Reasoning in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26029)
*Yu-Yang Chen,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TriViewBench——一个基于合成3D场景的三视角视觉推理基准，通过显式参数化物体数量和遮挡来控制结构复杂度。评估18个MLLM发现：所有模型能力层级一致（局部决策>物体计数>全局恢复），性能随复杂度单调下降，CoT几乎没有效果（-0.16%），计数任务存在单视图漏数和多视图重数的机制性分离错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在标准VQA基准上表现良好，但在可控结构复杂度下的可扩展性尚未被充分理解，缺乏一个能够系统诊断多视角空间推理瓶颈的受控基准。

Method: 构建TriViewBench：从合成3D场景生成三视图（左/中/右），显式参数化物体数量和遮挡程度；包含1923个场景和14K+ QA对，4个复杂度级别和3个推理类别（局部决策、物体计数、全局恢复）；在统一提示协议下评估18个开源和闭源MLLM。

Result: 所有18个模型表现出一致的能力层级：局部决策>物体计数>全局恢复。性能随复杂度单调下降（局部决策下降12.11%，物体计数下降59.14%，全局恢复崩溃80.02%）。计数错误存在机制性分离：单视图任务以遮挡导致的漏数为主，多视图任务以跨视图身份混淆导致的重数为主。CoT几乎无收益（-0.16%），且对全局恢复的效果受能力门槛限制。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM存在根本性的可扩展性限制，瓶颈在于跨视图空间表征而非推理策略；TriViewBench可作为诊断结构推理失败的控制框架。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) demonstrate strong performance on standard visual question answering benchmarks, yet their scalability under controlled structural complexity remains poorly understood. We introduce TriViewBench, a controlled three-view visual reasoning benchmark constructed from synthetic 3D scenes with explicitly parameterized object count and occlusion. The benchmark contains 1,923 scenes and over 14K Question-Answer (QA) pairs organized into four complexity levels and three reasoning categories: Local Decision, Object Counting, and Global Recovery. We evaluate 18 open- and closed-source MLLMs under a unified prompting protocol. All 18 models exhibit an identical capability hierarchy without exception (Local Decision > Object Counting > Global Recovery), and performance degrades monotonically with complexity: Local Decision tasks decline modestly (12.11% relative drop), while Object Counting degrades substantially (59.14%) and Global Recovery collapses severely (80.02%). Error analysis on Object Counting reveals two mechanistically independent failure modes: single-view tasks are dominated by undercounting due to occlusion blindness, whereas the multi-view task reverses to overcounting due to cross-view identity confusion. Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting yields near-zero overall benefit ($Δ= -0.16\%$) and its effect on Global Recovery is strongly capability-gated, suggesting that the bottleneck lies in cross-view spatial representation rather than reasoning strategy. These findings reveal fundamental scalability limitations in current MLLMs and position TriViewBench as a controlled diagnostic framework for analyzing structural reasoning failures.

</details>


### [89] [How Robust is OCR-Reasoning? Evaluating OCR-Reasoning Robustness of Vision-Language Models under Visual Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26041)
*Yuxing Cheng,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了OCR-Robust基准，系统评估视觉语言模型在视觉扰动下的OCR推理鲁棒性，包含OCR1.0和OCR2.0两个子集、5种扰动类型和3个严重等级，使用多项鲁棒性指标评估了18个模型，发现高清洁准确率不意味着强鲁棒性，图表和表格比文档类输入更脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM在OCR基准上表现强劲，但在受控视觉退化下的鲁棒性尚未被充分理解，这对OCR推理任务中视觉损坏引发的错误和结构失真尤为关键。

Method: 构建包含812个样本的OCR-Robust基准，含OCR1.0（文档、场景文字、收据、手写、数学）和OCR2.0（图表、几何图、表格）两个子集；通过预实验从18种候选扰动中筛选出5种代表性类型，每种3个严重等级；使用清洁准确率、RCR、WCR和CRI四项指标评估18个模型（含闭源、开源VLM及OCR+LLM流水线）。

Result: 高清洁准确率不一定意味着强鲁棒性；模型在结构敏感的OCR任务上最坏情况下降严重；图表和表格比文档类输入对扰动更脆弱。

Conclusion: 现有VLM在OCR推理鲁棒性方面存在显著不足，尤其对结构化视觉内容（图表、表格）的扰动抵抗能力弱，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on OCR-based benchmarks and increasingly focused on text-rich understanding, but their robustness under controlled visual degradation remains insufficiently understood. This gap is critical for OCR reasoning, where visual corruption can induce OCR errors and structural distortions, thereby introducing uncertainty into the reasoning task. To systematically study this problem, we introduce OCR-Robust, a benchmark designed for evaluating OCR reasoning robustness under visual perturbations. It contains 812 samples across two complementary subsets: OCR1.0, covering documents, scene text, receipts, handwriting, and mathematical content, and OCR2.0, focusing on charts, geometry diagrams, and tables. To enable efficient yet informative evaluation, we conduct a pilot study over 18 candidate perturbations and select 5 representative types at 3 severity levels each based on their impact and cross-model discriminability. We evaluate robustness using clean accuracy, Relative Corruption Retention (RCR), Worst-Case Retention (WCR), and a composite Corruption Robustness Index (CRI), and benchmark 18 models spanning proprietary systems, open-source VLMs, and OCR+LLM pipelines. Our results show that higher clean accuracy does not necessarily imply stronger robustness, and that models can suffer pronounced degradation in the worst case on OCR tasks that are sensitive to structure, and charts and tables are substantially more fragile than document-like inputs under perturbation.

</details>


### [90] [DomainShuttle: Freeform Open Domain Subject-driven Text-to-video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26058)
*Nan Chen,Yiyang Cai,Rongchang Xie,Junwen Pan,Cheng Chen,Weinan Jia,Zhuowei Chen,Wen Zhou,Zhenbang Sun,Wenhan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了DomainShuttle方法，通过Domain-MoT（域感知自适应层归一化解耦视频与参考特征）、Video-Reference DualRoPE（将参考图像与视频令牌置于独立RoPE空间实现精确主体级空间建模）和Cross-Pair Consistent Loss（提取不受无关特征影响的主体特征），实现开放域主题驱动文本到视频生成在域内高保真与跨域高灵活性的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放域主题驱动文生视频方法主要关注域内场景的主体保真度，限制了在跨域场景（如新风格、语义组合、域属性）中的可编辑性和适应性；理想方法应在域内和跨域场景中均表现优异。

Method: 1) Domain-MoT：解耦视频与参考特征，引入域感知AdaLN进行特定域建模；2) Video-Reference DualRoPE：将参考图像令牌和视频令牌置于独立RoPE空间，实现精确主体级空间建模；3) Cross-Pair Consistent Loss：提取不受无关特征影响的主体固有特征。

Result: 实验表明DomainShuttle在多种开放域应用场景中相比现有方法实现了显著性能提升，兼具高主体保真度和生成灵活性。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能够灵活穿梭于不同域，在域内和跨域场景中均实现强性能，为开放域视频个性化提供了高保真与灵活生成兼具的解决方案。

Abstract: Open domain subject-driven text-to-video (S2V) generation has drawn significant interest in academia and industry. Open domain S2V mainly involves two scenarios: in-domain, which requires retaining the reference subject features as much as possible, and cross-domain, which preserves the intrinsic features of the subject while allowing subject-irrelevant properties to vary flexibly according to the text prompt. Existing methods primarily focus on maximizing subject fidelity in in-domain scenarios, which limits their editability and adaptability in cross-domain scenarios, such as novel styles, semantic combinations, or domain attributes. In this study, we propose that an ideal S2V method should flexibly shuttle between different domains, achieving strong performance in both in-domain and cross-domain scenarios. To this end, we propose DomainShuttle, which could achieve high fidelity and generative flexibility for open domain video personalization. Specifically, we introduce Domain-MoT, which decouples videos and reference features and introduces the domain-aware AdaLN for domain-specific modeling of reference images. We then introduce the Video-Reference DualRoPE scheme, which places reference image tokens and video tokens in separate RoPE spaces to enable precise subject-level spatial modeling, and Cross-Pair Consistent Loss, which aims to extract intrinsic subject features unaffected by irrelevant features. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DomainShuttle achieves significant performance improvements over existing methods, exhibiting high subject fidelity and generative flexibility across diverse open domain application scenarios.

</details>


### [91] [A welding penetration prediction model for laser welding process based on self-supervised learning using physics-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26059)
*Sen Li,Xiaoying Liu,Xiaojian Xu,Chendong Shao,Yaqi Wang,Ling Lan,Xinhua Tang,Haichao Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimPhysNet是一种将物理先验嵌入对比学习框架的自监督学习算法，仅需200张标注图像（占总标注集的5%）即可实现96.06%的激光焊接穿透状态分类准确率，性能与使用全部标注数据的传统监督学习方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光焊接全熔透状态是获得无缺陷焊接接头的关键因素，但现有的监督学习分类算法依赖大量高质量标注数据，在工业应用中受到限制。论文旨在利用少量标注数据实现高精度的焊接穿透状态预测。

Method: 提出SimPhysNet算法，核心是自监督对比学习框架嵌入物理先验（PINN引导提取熔池和匙孔的物理特征），结合三种图像增强任务提高泛化能力，最后基于原型网络（prototypical networks）的小样本学习策略实现鲁棒分类。

Result: 仅用200张标注图像（约5%标注数据）即达到96.06%的分类准确率，性能与使用全部标注数据的传统监督学习算法相当。

Conclusion: 该工作为激光焊接智能化自动化提供了一种高效、高精度且减少标注依赖的新方法。

Abstract: The laser welding full-penetration is of critical importance, as it constitutes one of the fundamental factors in achieving defect-free welded joints. Accurate prediction of the penetration state is therefore essential for ensuring weld quality. To this end, this paper introduces SimPhysNet, a novel algorithm that achieves high classification accuracy in laser welding penetration prediction using only a limited number of labelled images. This approach effectively overcomes the limitations of supervised learning classification algorithms, which are hindered in industrial applications by their dependence on extensive, high-quality labelled data. The core of SimPhysNet is a unique self-supervised learning paradigm that embeds physical priors into a contrastive learning framework. By incorporating a physics-informed neural network (PINN), the model is guided to extract physically meaningful features of the molten pool and keyhole from a large set of unlabelled data, while three image augmentation tasks further enhance its generalization capabilities. Subsequently, a few-shot learning strategy, based on prototypical networks, enables robust classification by constructing class representations from a minimal set of labelled images. Experimental results demonstrate that SimPhysNet achieves a classification accuracy of 96.06% using only 200 labelled images (approximately 5% of the total labelled dataset), which is comparable to the performance of conventional supervised learning algorithms that utilize the entire labelled dataset. This work presents a new, efficient, and highly accurate method, providing the way for the intelligent automation of laser welding.

</details>


### [92] [A cross-process welding penetration status prediction algorithm based on unsupervised domain adaptation in laser and TIG welding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26078)
*Sen Li,Haichao Cui,Chendong Shao,Yaqi Wang,Xinhua Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合逐步源域扩展（GSDE）的无监督域自适应（UDA）框架，用于解决不同焊接工艺（如TIG与激光焊接）间因物理机制差异导致的域偏移问题，在跨工艺迁移任务中大幅超越监督基线（提升43%+），并降低重新标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有监督深度学习在焊接熔透状态分类中表现优异，但跨工艺（如TIG到激光焊接）时因物理机制差异（电弧vs.锁孔）导致性能严重下降，重新标注成本高，亟需无监督域自适应方法。

Method: 提出无监督域自适应（UDA）框架，结合逐步源域扩展（GSDE）策略，在TIG和激光焊接数据集上进行同工艺和跨工艺迁移评估。

Result: 同工艺平均精度90.65%（TIG）和90.72%（激光），超监督基线35.83%和38.87%；跨工艺TIG→激光80.48%，激光→TIG81.13%，超基线43.39%和43.40%。UMAP可视化验证模型学到域不变特征并保持类别判别边界。

Conclusion: 该方法显著降低了新焊接工艺的重新标注成本，提升了智能监控系统在不同焊接系统中的通用性。

Abstract: Supervised deep learning has been widely used for weld penetration state classification; however, its performance often degrades significantly under domain shift, such as when transferring models between welding processes with distinct physical mechanisms:for instance, from arc-dominated tungsten inert gas (TIG) welding to keyhole-based laser welding. To overcome this limitation, we propose an unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) framework integrated with a gradual source domain expansion (GSDE) strategy. Evaluated on dedicated TIG and laser welding datasets, our approach achieves high accuracy in both same-process and cross-process transfer tasks. Specifically, it attains average accuracies of 90.65% on TIGFH and 90.72% on LSPS in same-process settings, surpassing a supervised baseline by 35.83% and 38.87%, respectively. More notably, in cross-process scenarios, it reaches 80.48% for TIG to Laser and 81.13% for Laser to TIG, improving upon the baseline by 43.39% and 43.40%. UMAP visualizations verify that the model learns domain-invariant features while maintaining discriminative class boundaries. This method considerably lowers the relabeling cost for new welding processes and enhances the versatility of intelligent monitoring across different welding systems.

</details>


### [93] [MVTrack4Gen: Multi-View Point Tracking as Geometric Supervision for 4D Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26087)
*JoungBin Lee,Jaewoo Jung,Jongmin Lee,Tongmin Kim,Hyunsung Kim,Takuya Narihira,Kazumi Fukuda,Jahyeok Koo,Jisang Han,Yuki Mitsufuji,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MVTrack4Gen，一种利用多视图点跟踪作为几何和运动监督信号的训练框架，增强相机条件式新视图视频扩散模型中的注意力层对应关系，以实现更好的几何一致性和运动保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于显式3D表示的方法受限于单目视频动态对象几何重建不准确，而仅基于相机条件的方法虽视觉质量高但难以保持几何和运动一致性。

Method: 发现特定注意力层编码强对应线索（查询特征在跨视图和时间上关注几何对应位置的关键特征），将这些特征路由到辅助多视图跟踪头，联合训练扩散模型与点跟踪损失，强化运动感知对应关系。

Result: 在多个基准上实现最先进的几何一致性和有竞争力的相机精度。

Conclusion: 多视图点跟踪作为额外监督信号能有效提升相机条件式新视图视频扩散模型的几何与运动一致性。

Abstract: Synthesizing a novel-view video from a monocular reference video along a target camera trajectory requires both geometric consistency and motion fidelity with respect to the reference video. Existing methods based on explicit 3D representations are limited by the accuracy of off-the-shelf reconstruction modules, which often produce inaccurate geometry for dynamic objects in monocular videos. In contrast, camera-conditioning-only methods can achieve high visual quality but often struggle to preserve geometric and motion consistency. In this work, we introduce MVTrack4Gen (Multi-View point Tracking for Novel-View Generation), a motion-aware training framework that leverages multi-view point tracking as an additional geometric and motion supervision signal for camera-conditioning-only novel-view video diffusion models. Our key finding is that specific attention layers encode strong correspondence cues, where query features attend to key features at geometrically corresponding locations across views and over time, and the misalignment of these correspondences causes motion inconsistency. Based on this observation, we route these features into an auxiliary multi-view tracking head and jointly train the diffusion model with a point-tracking objective. By explicitly strengthening these motion-aware correspondences, MVTrack4Gen improves existing models to better follow the motion in the reference view and maintain cross-view geometric consistency. Across diverse benchmarks, our method achieves state-of-the-art geometric consistency and competitive camera accuracy.

</details>


### [94] [TryOnCrafter: Unleashing Camera Trajectories for Realistic Video Virtual Try-on via a Renderable 4D Try-on Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26092)
*Hao Sun,Hao Yan,Mengting Chen,Quanjian Song,Yu Li,Juan Cao,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Bo Zheng,Sheng Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CaM-VVT（相机可控视频虚拟试穿）新任务及首个统一框架TryOnCrafter，通过可渲染4D试穿代理（基于3DGS+SMPL-X）显式解耦人体与环境，实现任意相机轨迹下的逼真视频生成，并支持人体重新定位、子弹时间、360度环绕等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚拟试穿（VVT）被动依赖源相机轨迹，无法支持用户自由探索任意视角，限制了交互自由度。

Method: 提出TryOnCrafter框架：1）构建Renderable 4D Try-on Proxy，蒸馏2D试穿先验到带衣物3DGS化身，用SMPL-X动画并度量对齐到背景点云；2）Proxy-Anchored Video DiT以该代理为几何锚点，约束生成视频符合指定轨迹和物理形变。

Result: 实现首个CaM-VVT任务框架，支持视角可控的虚拟试穿视频生成，具备4D代理的可编辑性，可做人体重新定位、子弹时间、360度环绕观看。

Conclusion: 通过显式4D代理解耦人体与环境，突破了传统VVT对源轨迹的被动依赖，为交互式虚拟试穿开辟了新方向。

Abstract: While Video Virtual Try-on (VVT) has achieved remarkable progress in synthesizing realistic garment overlays on dynamic subjects, existing paradigms remains fundamentally constrained by a passive dependency on source camera trajectories, failing to accommodate the requisite interactive freedom for omnidirectional viewpoint exploration. To address this limitation, we define a pioneering research frontier: Camera-controllable Video Virtual Try-on (CaM-VVT). Unlike conventional VVT, CaM-VVT not only necessitates viewpoint-agnostic texture hallucination but also strict structural synchronization between non-rigid human dynamics and background contexts under arbitrary, unconstrained camera movements. To tackle these challenges, we present TryOnCrafter, the first unified DiT-based framework specifically architected for the CaM-VVT task. Departing from implicit pixel-space manipulation, we introduce a Renderable 4D Try-on Proxy that explicitly decouples the human subject from the environment. This is achieved by distilling high-fidelity 2D try-on priors into a clothed 3DGS-based avatar, which is subsequently animated via SMPL-X sequences and metric-aligned into a reconstructed background point cloud. This proxy establishes a robust structural foundation with superior texture density and motion integrity. Our Proxy-Anchored Video DiT leverages this robust structural foundation as a primary geometric anchor, ensuring that the synthesized photorealistic videos are strictly constrained by prescribed trajectories and physically plausible deformations. Benefiting from the inherent editability of the 4D proxy, TryOnCrafter facilitates diverse downstream applications, including human relocalization, ``bullet time'' effects, and $360$-degree orbital viewing.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [BFMTrack: Latent Sequence Optimization for Physics-Based Motion Tracking with Behavioral Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25056)
*Thomas Rupf,Agon Serifi,David Müller,Sammy Christen,Ruben Grandia,Espen Knoop,Moritz Bächer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Latent Sequence Optimization (LSO)，通过策略梯度优化潜在序列，扩展行为基础模型(BFM)的能力以支持精确运动跟踪，无需奖励工程，并在密集跟踪、稀疏关键帧和真实人形机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为基础模型(BFM)擅长时不变任务（如到达目标、基于状态的奖励优化），但其潜在空间无法直接支持时变目标（如运动序列跟踪）。现有的移动窗口平均启发式方法无法捕捉高度动态运动的细微变化。

Method: 提出Latent Sequence Optimization (LSO)，将模拟 rollout 与策略梯度更新结合，优化潜在序列；使用时间相关噪声对潜在序列建模以引导优化朝向平滑、连贯的潜在轨迹。

Result: 在密集跟踪、稀疏关键帧任务上验证有效性，并成功部署到真实人形机器人上。

Conclusion: LSO扩展了BFM的能力，实现精确运动跟踪，无需奖励工程和调参，且能直接部署到真实机器人。

Abstract: Behavioral Foundation Models (BFMs) offer a promising path toward universal physics-based character control by organizing a rich repertoire of physically plausible behaviors into a latent space, guided by a large-scale motion dataset. While these models excel at time-invariant tasks, such as goal-reaching and state-based reward optimization, their latent space does not directly support time-varying objectives, such as tracking a motion sequence. For tracking, existing heuristics rely on moving-window-averaging that fails to capture the nuances of highly dynamic motions. In this work, we propose a novel Latent Sequence Optimization (LSO) to address these shortcomings. Our approach combines simulation rollouts with a policy gradient update to optimize over a sequence of latents, extending the capabilities of BFMs toward precise motion tracking without requiring reward engineering and tuning. To guide the optimization toward smooth, coherent latent trajectories, we model the latent sequence using temporally correlated noise. We validate our approach across dense tracking, sparse keyframing, and direct deployment onto a real humanoid robot.

</details>


### [96] [Invariant Kalman filtering for extended pose estimation in multi-IMU articulated rigid-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25083)
*Sven Goffin,Cédric Schwartz,Silvère Bonnabel,Olivier Brüls,Pierre Sacré*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对IMU惯导铰接刚体系统的扩展位姿估计问题，提出相对L-扩展位姿的李群表示，将关节运动学约束以不变形式融入迭代IEKF框架，在UR5e机器人和人腿实验上相比基线EKF/IterEKF等降低至少50%的RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IEKF对单刚体位姿估计有收敛保证和一致性，但将其扩展到铰接系统时，刚体间位姿耦合和关节运动学约束的融入是开放问题。

Method: 提出相对L-扩展位姿（Lie群表示），使多IMU铰接系统具有群仿射动力学；将关节约束建模为无噪声伪观测，在迭代IEKF中融入，保持不变滤波的收敛和一致性保证。

Result: 在UR5e机械臂和人腿上验证，相比所有EKF/IterEKF/绝对位姿IterIEKF基线，收敛更快、变异性更低、RMSE降低至少50%。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效将IEKF的收敛和一致性保证扩展到铰接刚体系统，在机器人及人体运动估计任务上显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Accurate extended pose estimation (orientation, velocity, and position) for IMU-instrumented articulated rigid-body systems is a key challenge in robotics and human motion analysis. The invariant extended Kalman filter (IEKF) addresses this problem for a single rigid body with convergence guarantees and consistency under unobservability, but extending these properties to articulated systems is nontrivial: inter-body pose coupling prevents a direct application, and incorporating joint kinematic constraints within the invariant framework remains an open problem. To address this gap, we introduce the relative L-extended pose, a Lie group representation for kinematic-tree systems. With one IMU per body, it yields group-affine dynamics and allows joint constraints to be expressed in invariant form. We incorporate these constraints as noise-free pseudo-measurements within an iterated IEKF (IterIEKF), thereby preserving the convergence and consistency guarantees of invariant filtering. Validated on both a UR5e robot and a human leg, the proposed IterIEKF outperforms all EKF, IterEKF, and absolute-pose IterIEKF baselines. It converges faster, exhibits lower run-to-run variability, and consistently achieves the lowest RMSE, with reductions of at least 50% compared to the second-best filter across all scenarios considered in this work.

</details>


### [97] [ADM-Fusion: Adaptive Deep Multi-Sensor Fusion for Robust Ego-Motion Estimation in Diverse Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25111)
*Hasan Moughnieh,Ibrahim Ghaddar,Hadi Elham,Imad H. Elhajj,Daniel Asmar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ADM-Fusion，一种端到端深度学习的多传感器融合方法，采用自适应传感器混合专家框架和内容感知路由动态分配传感器权重，并引入交叉任务注意力机制分离平移与旋转分支，在CARLA-LOC模拟训练后微调至KITTI真实数据，在退化条件下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多传感器融合在退化环境下传感器可靠性波动大，传统固定权重融合无法适应不同模态的差异化失效模式，需要自适应平衡互补线索以实现可靠的自主系统。

Method: 采用自适应传感器混合专家(MoE)框架实现内容感知路由动态分配传感器权重；设计分离的平移与旋转分支，通过交叉任务注意力机制保持任务特异性并促进信息共享；模拟训练后微调至真实数据的sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 在退化条件下保持鲁棒性，与现有方法相比具有竞争力的性能，验证了从模拟到真实的迁移有效性。

Conclusion: 自适应传感器融合是提升退化环境下自主系统可靠性的有效途径，MoE框架和任务分支分离设计具有实际价值。

Abstract: Robust multi-sensor fusion is essential for reliable autonomy in diverse and degraded environments, where sensor reliability can fluctuate rapidly. Because different modalities fail in distinct ways, effective fusion should adaptively balance complementary cues rather than rely on fixed weighting. This adaptability is particularly important for ego-motion estimation, since accurate updates depend on the consistent integration of complementary sensor information. We propose ADM-Fusion, an end-to-end deep learning based multi-sensor fusion method designed to adapt to environmental changes and sensor degradation. ADM-Fusion employs an adaptive sensor mixture-of-experts framework with content-aware routing to dynamically assign weights to sensor inputs in real time. The system further incorporates separate translation and rotation branches, coupled through a cross-task attention mechanism to preserve task-specific specialization while enabling information sharing. ADM-Fusion is trained on the CARLA-LOC simulated dataset and subsequently fine-tuned on KITTI real-world data, demonstrating effective simulation-to-real transfer. Experiments show that ADM-Fusion remains robust under degraded conditions while maintaining competitive performance against existing methods.

</details>


### [98] [SurveilNav: Collaborative Object Goal Navigation with Robot and Surveillance System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25119)
*Ming-Ming Yu,Qunbo Wang,Rongtao Xu,Yanghong Mei,Yirong Yang,Longteng Guo,Wenjun Wu,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SurveilNav，一种多视角监控与机器人协同导航框架，通过主动相机调度、联合2D/3D建图、VLM价值估计和协同目标验证，解决单机器人感知范围有限和固定相机盲区问题，在HM3D数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多聚焦于单机器人场景，缺乏多视角协作能力，无法充分利用大规模环境中的固定监控网络信息，导致探索效率低下。

Method: SurveilNav框架包含四个模块：主动相机调度（选择最优监控视角）、联合2D/3D建图（融合机器人与监控信息）、VLM价值估计（评估探索收益）、协同目标验证（多视角确认目标）。

Result: 在HM3D数据集上显著优于现有方法，在探索效率和导航成功率上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 监控-机器人协同导航是一种有效的范式，在大规模搜索、家庭环境和救援任务中有广阔应用前景。

Abstract: With the growing deployment of surveillance systems in factories, offices, and homes, integrating them with robots offers a promising direction for collaborative and efficient task execution. However, existing approaches largely focus on single-robot scenarios and struggle with multi-view collaboration in large-scale environments. In this paper, we present a novel indoor collaborative object navigation dataset built on Habitat-Sim, featuring 206 cameras across 74 floors. The dataset enables systematic evaluation of an agent's ability to exploit multi-view surveillance information. To address the limitations of single-robot perception, we propose SurveilNav, a collaborative navigation framework that integrates active camera scheduling, joint 2D/3D mapping, VLM-based value estimation, and collaborative target verification. By synergizing the robot's dynamic local perception with the static global view of surveillance, this architecture effectively overcomes both the limited perception range of single agents and the inherent blind spots of fixed cameras, resolving inefficient exploration. Experimental results on the HM3D dataset demonstrate that SurveilNav substantially outperforms existing methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance in both exploration efficiency and navigation success rate. Moreover, the system shows strong potential for applications in large-scale search, home environments, and rescue missions.

</details>


### [99] [AeroCast: Probabilistic 3D Trajectory Prediction for Non-Cooperative Aerial Obstacles via Transformer-MDN Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25122)
*Syed Izzat Ullah,Jose Baca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AeroCast是一个结合Transformer编码器与混合密度网络的概率轨迹预测框架，用于无人机在共享空域中对非合作障碍物（如鸟类、失控无人机）进行多模态运动预测，在5秒预测窗口内将ADE和FDE降低约50%，推理时间仅0.1ms，支持100Hz实时机载部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么使用递归编码器顺序传播时间信息，难以捕捉机动起始的长程运动学前兆；要么产生点预测，无法为下游规划器提供分布信息。需要一种能表示非合作障碍物多模态运动的概率预测方法。

Method: 结合Transformer编码器（捕捉长程时序依赖）与MDN输出头（预测每时间步的未来3D位移高斯混合分布）；提出平移不变的连续位移编码处理输入；设计校准导向的训练目标解决混合分布的模式退化问题。

Result: 在包含9种运动类别的混合真实/合成四旋翼数据集上，5秒预测窗口内ADE和FDE相对基线降低约50%；NLL和CRPS均最优；消融实验确认速度输入和模型容量是主要贡献因素，位置编码对长程轨迹一致性至关重要；推理时间0.1ms/样本。

Conclusion: AeroCast在非合作空中障碍物概率轨迹预测中达到了SOTA精度与实时推理速度，适合机载100Hz实时部署。

Abstract: Autonomous aerial vehicles operating in shared airspace must predict the future positions of non-cooperative obstacles to plan evasive maneuvers before a collision becomes unavoidable. Unlike cooperative systems that share intent, non-cooperative obstacles such as birds, uncontrolled drones, or debris exhibit multi-modal motion that deterministic predictors cannot adequately represent. Existing methods either rely on recurrent encoders that propagate temporal information sequentially, limiting their ability to capture long-range kinematic precursors of maneuver initiation, or produce point forecasts that provide no distributional information to downstream planners. This paper presents AeroCast, a probabilistic trajectory prediction framework that combines a Transformer encoder with a Mixture Density Network output head to predict per-timestep Gaussian mixture distributions over future three-dimensional displacements. A translation-invariant consecutive displacement encoding and a calibration-oriented training objective address the input design and mode-degeneracy challenges specific to mixture-based aerial trajectory prediction. On a hybrid real-and-synthetic quadrotor corpus spanning nine motion categories, AeroCast reduces Average Displacement Error and Final Displacement Error by approximately 50% relative to the baselines over a five-second horizon, and achieves the lowest negative log-likelihood and Continuous Ranked Probability Score among all compared methods. Ablation analysis identifies velocity input and model capacity as the primary contributors to prediction quality, and positional encoding as essential for long-horizon trajectory coherence. AeroCast inference completes in 0.1ms per sample, compatible with real-time onboard deployment at 100Hz.

</details>


### [100] [RGB: RL Guided Whole-Body MPPI for Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25123)
*Yunsoo Seo,Sol Choi,Euncheol Im,Myo Taeg Lim,Yisoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种RL引导的全身模型预测路径积分(MPPI)框架，将预训练RL策略作为采样先验，MPPI作为附加反馈控制器在线修正RL先验以满足任务目标，无需重新训练策略。在29-DoF Unitree G1人形机器人上实现平均280Hz的高速率控制，相比纯RL基线提升了任务级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度RL虽然能实现鲁棒的稳定性，但其行为与训练目标和命令接口紧密耦合，添加新的反馈目标需要重新训练。现有方法难以灵活地在不重新训练的情况下引入新的目标任务约束。

Method: 将预训练RL策略作为MPPI的采样先验（sampling prior），引导MPPI的 rollout 趋向动态可行的行为；通过模块化的MPPI代价项指定任务目标，MPPI在线连续修正RL先验以满足目标，无需重新训练策略。

Result: 在29-DoF Unitree G1人形机器人MuJoCo仿真中实现平均280Hz的高速率控制；在相同命令接口下相比纯RL基线提升了任务级精度，能够纠正直行行走中的系统性偏移并跟踪额外的全身参考信号。

Conclusion: RL+MPPI的混合框架有效结合了RL的鲁棒性和MPPI的灵活目标修正能力，在不重新训练策略的前提下实现了更好的任务级控制精度，为人形机器人全身控制提供了可行的增量改进方案。

Abstract: Humanoid robots require whole-body controllers that are both robust and precise in contact-rich environments. While deep reinforcement learning (RL) achieves robust stability, its behavior is tightly coupled to the training objective and command interface, making it difficult to add new feedback objectives without retraining. In this study, we propose an RL guided whole-body model predictive path integral (MPPI) framework that acts as an add-on feedback controller on top of a pretrained RL policy. Instead of using RL policy as the final controller, we use it as a sampling prior that biases MPPI rollouts toward dynamically feasible behaviors. Task objectives are specified through modular MPPI cost terms, and MPPI closes the loop by continuously correcting the RL prior online to satisfy these objectives without retraining the policy. Simulations on a 29-DoF Unitree G1 humanoid in MuJoCo demonstrate stable high-rate control (average 280~Hz). The proposed method improves task-level precision over a pure RL baseline under the same command interface. This is achieved by correcting systematic drift during straight walking and tracking additional whole-body reference signals imposed through the cost.

</details>


### [101] [Causality-Based Parametric Control Barrier Function for Safe Multi-Vehicle Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25134)
*Yiwei Lyu,Caleb Chang,John M. Dolan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于因果推断的参数化控制屏障函数（Causality-based Parametric-CBF），用于多车交互场景中显式推理车辆间的相互影响，从而构建自适应安全控制器，避免过度保守行为，提升多车系统的任务效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全控制方法在多车交互中存在两个问题：1）难以从观测数据中确定周围车辆行为的因果关系（受车辆间相互影响干扰）；2）采用最坏情况分析导致控制器过于保守。需要一种能够显式推理车辆间因果影响且不过度保守的方法。

Method: 将Parametric-CBF扩展到多机器人交互场景，嵌入因果推断机制，显式建模周围车辆行为与自车动作之间的因果关系，学习基于因果关系的参数化控制屏障函数，并据此设计自适应安全控制器。

Result: 在多种高交互强度的多车场景中，利用多车系统的运动灵活性，任务效率得到显著提升。

Conclusion: 因果推断嵌入CBF可以有效解决多车交互中的因果混淆问题，避免过度保守，提升任务效率。

Abstract: Safe control has been widely studied in various safety-critical applications, for instance, autonomous driving. In order to ensure the autonomous vehicle does not collide with other vehicles, it is essential to obtain an accurate expectation of surrounding vehicles' behavior and react adaptively. Instead of assuming fully cooperative and homogeneous vehicles using the same safety-critical controllers, recent works have been exploring different data-driven approaches to model the neighboring vehicles' underlying controllers with observed data. However, existing works either suffer from 1) the inter-vehicle influence during the multi-vehicle interaction, which makes it hard to determine the causality of surrounding vehicles' behavior in controller modeling, or 2) being dominated by the worst-case analysis, which may lead to overly conservative behavior. In this paper, we extend the prior work on Parametric-Control Barrier Function (Parametric-CBF) to multi-robot interactions with embedded causality inference to explicitly reason over the inter-vehicle influence. Given the learned Causality-based Parametric-CBF, we present an adaptive safety-critical controller that allows the ego vehicle to safely react to surrounding vehicles with the learned expectation. We demonstrate that by leveraging the motion flexibility among multi-vehicle systems, task efficiency can be greatly improved in various interaction-intensive scenarios.

</details>


### [102] [Memory Retrieval in Visuomotor Policies for Long-Horizon Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25136)
*Rutav Shah,Yisu Li,Femi Bello,Yuke Zhu,Roberto Martín-Martín*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HALO是一种使用注意力记忆检索机制的视觉运动策略，通过蒸馏VLM先验抑制虚假相关性，并利用稀疏注意力减少记忆累积误差，实现了从8分钟历史经验中检索任务相关信息的长时程家庭机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人在部分可观测的家庭环境中需要记忆来支持自主性，但现有Transformer记忆检索机制直接用于模仿学习存在两个问题：策略学到历史信息与动作间的虚假相关性，以及预测误差累积导致模型漂移和级联故障。

Method: HALO包含两个核心设计：(1) 将VLM先验蒸馏到策略中，通过从示范轨迹生成记忆相关的问答对并联合训练视频问答目标，引导检索关注任务相关信息；(2) 使用稀疏注意力限制检索仅访问历史中最相关的部分，减少闭环控制中累积误差的影响。

Result: HALO实现了从长达8分钟的过去经验中检索任务相关信息，实现更可靠的长时程控制，有效解决了虚假相关性和累积误差两个挑战。

Conclusion: HALO通过VLM蒸馏和稀疏注意力机制，为家庭机器人提供了一种通用且可靠的记忆检索方法，在长时程自主控制中具有显著优势。

Abstract: General-purpose robots operating in partially observable environments, such as homes, require memory to support autonomy. They must recall diverse information from the past, such as where objects were placed, which tasks a human partner has completed, and when an appliance was turned on. Achieving this versatility requires a general memory retrieval mechanism. Transformer architectures that use attention over long contexts for memory retrieval provide a promising approach, as they learn retrieval from data rather than relying on task-specific or hand-designed rules. However, directly incorporating them into imitation learning from offline data introduces two key challenges: (1) the policy may learn spurious correlations between past information and predicted actions, and (2) errors accumulate in memory due to prediction inaccuracies and their compounding interactions with the environment, causing model drift and cascading failures. To address both challenges, we introduce HALO, a visuomotor policy with an attention-based memory retrieval mechanism for long-horizon control. First, to suppress spurious correlations, HALO distills vision-language model (VLM) priors into the policy. It generates memory-dependent question--answer pairs from demonstration trajectories and trains jointly with a video question--answering objective, steering retrieval toward task-relevant information. Second, to reduce the impact of accumulated errors in memory during closed-loop control, HALO uses sparse attention that restricts retrieval to only the most relevant parts of the history. Together, these components enable more reliable long-horizon control by guiding the policy to retrieve task-relevant information from up to eight minutes of past experience. Project website: https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/HALO

</details>


### [103] [SwarmFly: A simulation platform for UAV swarm experiment design and validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25146)
*Abhishek Phadke,Karthik Kumar Vasudeva,Abhishek Joshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SwarmFly 是一个基于 MATLAB 的开源多无人机蜂群仿真与测试平台，提供四种协调模式、IMU 遥测、IP 地理定位和插件架构，并通过八项实验验证了编队精度、抗风性、故障恢复、能耗和空域合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源无人机蜂群仿真工具普遍存在维护不足、学习曲线陡峭、场景单一等问题，缺乏一个全面模块化的仿真平台，这是公认的研究空白。

Method: SwarmFly 采用 MATLAB 实现，集成实时作战地图、四种蜂群协调模式（领航-跟随、分散式、异构中继、异构速度）、模拟 IMU 遥测和基于 IP 的地理定位，并设计插件架构允许研究人员在不修改核心代码的情况下添加行为、故障模型和分析工具，内置八个插件扩展为完整测试平台。

Result: 通过八个实验分別验证了编队精度、抗风能力、故障恢复、能量续航和空域合规性等子系统性能，证明了平台的有效性和模块化可扩展性。

Conclusion: SwarmFly 提供了一个模块化、可扩展的 MATLAB 仿真平台，支持向硬件在环测试、更大规模和更高保真度动力学扩展，已开源发布。

Abstract: The initial development phase of UAV swarms largely depends on simulation for experimental design and validation, yet existing open-source tools are often unmaintained, have steep learning curves, or are built around a single fixed scenario. The need for a comprehensive, modular simulation platform is a recognized research gap. This paper presents SwarmFly, a MATLAB-based simulation and test platform for multi- UAV swarms that addresses these gaps. SwarmFly combines a real-time operational map, four swarm coordination modes (leader-follower, decentralized, heterogeneous relay, and heterogeneous speed), simulated IMU telemetry, and IP-based geolocation with a plugin architecture that lets researchers add behaviors, fault models, and analysis tools without touching the core code. Eight bundled plugins extend the base simulator into a full test harness. The SwarmFly platform exposes multi-agent aerial swarms to a wide range of internal and external disruptions, enabling observation and quantification of underlying swarm control and behavioral mechanisms. This study verifies and characterizes each subsystem through eight experiments that measure formation accuracy, wind tolerance, fault recovery, energy endurance, and airspace compliance. The platform runs entirely in MATLAB. Its modular design supports straightforward extension toward hardware-in-the-loop testing, larger swarms, and higher-fidelity dynamics. An open-source release is available at [https://github.com/abhishekphadke/SwarmFly.git]

</details>


### [104] [Toward Low-Latency Vision-Language Models with Doubly-Correct Predictions in Egocentric Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25160)
*Qitong Wang,Fan Du,Pranav Maneriker,Jihui Jin,Christopher Rasmussen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究VLM剪枝在双正确预测（准确+证据可靠）视角下的表现，发现现有方法常保留正确证据定位但破坏预测准确性，提出基于理由感知的剪枝策略以更好对齐证据与决策，在人-机器人协作的自我中心视频数据集上取得最优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人-机器人协作中VLM的低延迟推理至关重要，但现有剪枝方法在追求效率时忽视了"双正确预测"（既准确又具有证据支撑）的要求，这直接影响人机交互的安全性和信任度。

Method: 提出理由感知剪枝策略（rationale-informed pruning），在剪枝过程中将模型决策依据（evidence/rationale）与预测准确性对齐，而非仅关注精度或模型尺寸单一目标。

Result: 在自我中心视频基准上，所提方法在预测准确率和双正确预测指标上均超越现有剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 剪枝可以同时兼顾效率与可信赖性，理由感知对齐是确保VLM在机器人交互中安全可靠的关键设计原则。

Abstract: The rapid rise of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in egocentric visual understanding has made low-latency inference in human-robot collaborative (HRC) tasks increasingly critical. Weight pruning techniques developed for VLMs to shrink model size and computation can be readily applied to satisfy the efficiency demands of on-board processing and real-time interactive robotics. Moreover, safe human-robot interaction demands pruning strategies that preserve doubly-correct predictions; outputs must be both accurate and evidentially grounded to mitigate risks and ensure user trust. In this paper, we present a new study of VLM pruning through the lens of doubly-correct prediction. Our experiments surprisingly show that existing pruning methods often preserve the right evidence localization but undermine correct prediction. To address this, we propose a rationale-informed pruning strategy that better aligns evidence with decisions. Benchmark results on egocentric video datasets demonstrate that our method not only achieves the highest prediction accuracy but also outperforms existing approaches in attaining doubly-correct predictions. We aim to stimulate research on efficient and reliable VLMs, ensuring accuracy-driven advances align with the transparency, auditability, and safety required for responsible human-robot interaction and embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [105] [fARfetch: Enabling Collocated AR-HRC in Large Visually Diverse Environments with VLM-Driven AR Content Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25162)
*Christian Fronk,Hanting Ye,David Hunt,Miroslav Pajic,Maria Gorlatova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: fARfetch是一个AR-HRC系统，结合共享语义环境映射、上下文感知世界缩微图和VLM驱动的AR视图管理，在大规模室外环境中实现人与四足机器人的高效协作。用户研究(N=13)表明，相比非AR基线，任务完成时间提升66%，脑力、时间需求和挫败感分别降低43%、34%和66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强现实可改善人机协作，但大型、视觉多样的室外环境（长距离、超视距）对交互和内容可读性构成挑战，现有AR-HRC系统在室外大规模场景下的表现有限。

Method: (i) AR头显与机器人之间的共享语义环境映射，可视化检测到的地标以支持地标引导的导航命令；(ii) 上下文感知世界缩微图用于精细路径编辑；(iii) VLM驱动的AR视图管理，联合调整虚拟内容的颜色、大小和方向以保持可读性。在Meta Quest 3和Unitree Go2四足机器人上实现。

Result: 在30.5米真实室外检查任务中，fARfetch相比非AR基线：任务完成时间显著提升66%，脑力需求降低43%，时间需求降低34%，挫败感降低66%。可读性调查表明系统在大型室外环境中有效维持了虚拟内容可读性。

Conclusion: AR-HRC系统结合共享语义建图、世界缩微图和VLM驱动的视图管理，可显著提升室外大规模场景下的人机协作效率和用户体验，降低认知负荷。

Abstract: Augmented Reality (AR) can improve collocated human-robot collaboration by making robot state and intent visible and enabling intuitive control, yet large, visually diverse environments like the outdoors challenge both interaction and content legibility, especially at long distances and beyond visual line of sight. We present fARfetch, an AR-HRC system that integrates (i) shared semantic environment mapping across an AR headset and robot that visualizes detected landmarks in AR to support landmark-grounded go-to commands, (ii) a context-aware world-in-miniature representation of the shared environment for fine-grained path authoring, and (iii) vision-language-model driven AR view management that jointly adapts virtual content color, size, and orientation to maintain legibility in large visually diverse environments. We implement fARfetch with a Meta Quest 3 headset and Unitree Go2 quadruped robot, and conduct a within-subjects user study (N=13) on a real-world large-scale (30.5m) outdoor inspection task. fARfetch yielded significantly faster completion times than a non-AR baseline (66%) and significantly lower workload in mental demand (-43%), temporal demand (-34%), and frustration (-66%). A custom legibility survey indicated fARfetch effectively maintained virtual content legibility in the large outdoor environment.

</details>


### [106] [Learning Perceptive Platform Adaptive Locomotion Controllers for Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25179)
*David Rytz,Kim Tien Ly,Ioannis Havoutis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究如何将感知信息整合到形态感知强化学习架构中，实现四足机器人的跨形态通用控制，对比了盲策略、仅评论家感知（MorAL+）和全感知演员-评论家（PPAL）三种方案，发现评论家级联感知在鲁棒性和跟踪一致性上优于盲策略且比全感知策略更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人运动控制器要么针对单一形态训练，要么采用忽略实时感知的盲策略，导致跨形态泛化能力差；而全感知策略对噪声敏感。缺乏对感知信息应如何整合到形态感知强化学习架构中的系统研究。

Method: 基于MorAL框架，在多种参考四足形态上训练形态专化通用控制器，使用自适应地形课程。对比三种变体：盲基线、仅评论家感知（MorAL+）和全感知演员-评论家（PPAL）。在仿真（平坦/崎岖地形）和ANYmal硬件上评估。

Result: 仅评论家感知策略（MorAL+）在鲁棒性和跟踪一致性上优于盲基线，且在感知噪声下比全感知策略更稳定。

Conclusion: 感知的放置位置和课程设计是实现可扩展、形态感知运动的关键因素。

Abstract: Universal quadrupedal locomotion remains limited by the difficulty of integrating perception across diverse robot morphologies. State-of-the-art controllers rely on single-robot training or blind policies that omit real-time perception, leading to poor cross-embodiment generalization. Designing locomotion policies that remain robust across related quadruped morphologies while incorporating perception is challenging. Moreover, fully perceptive policies are often sensitive to noise, whereas blind controllers lack terrain awareness. In this work, we study how perception should be integrated into morphology-aware reinforcement learning architectures for deployable quadrupedal control. Building on MorAL, we train morphology-specialized universal controllers on multiple reference quadrupeds using adaptive terrain curricula. We compare a blind baseline, a critic-perceptive variant (MorAL+), and a fully perceptive actor-critic (PPAL). Policies are evaluated in simulation on flat and rough terrains, and deployed on ANYmal hardware. Results show that critic-only perception improves robustness and tracking consistency over blind baselines while remaining more stable than fully perceptive policies under perception noise. These findings highlight that perception placement and curriculum design are key factors for scalable, morphology-aware locomotion.

</details>


### [107] [RAVEN: Long-Horizon Reasoning & Navigation with a Visuo-Spatio-Temporal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25206)
*Yixun Hu,Zhicheng Zheng,Lihan Zha,Chunwei Xing,Rajdeep Singh,Omar Hossain,Antonio Loquercio,Dhruv Shah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RAVEN，一种基于视觉嵌入的智能体记忆系统，通过向量数据库存储位姿和时间信息，结合空间地图检索，实现长时域机器人问答与导航，无需图像转文字描述即可进行语义、空间和时间检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人记忆系统依赖图像转文字描述（captioning）存在信息损失，且在大规模长时域部署中缺乏紧凑、可扩展的记忆结构，无法同时保留细粒度视觉语义、空间和时间信息。

Method: 将视觉嵌入（visual embeddings）与位姿（pose）和时间戳存入向量数据库，检索时结合空间地图（spatial map）进行约束，直接基于视觉特征进行语义、空间和时间的联合检索。

Result: 在模拟和真实视频问答基准上超越基于文字描述的记忆系统，在长时域任务上匹配前沿VLM但检索成本降低10倍，并在Unitree Go1机器人上实现大型室内环境中的长时域自然语言导航部署。

Conclusion: RAVEN证明了直接基于视觉嵌入的记忆系统在长期机器人部署中的有效性，兼顾精度、效率和可扩展性。

Abstract: Long-term robot deployment requires a compact and scalable memory that preserves fine-grained visual semantics, grounds observations in space and time, and enables efficient storage and retrieval. In this paper, we propose RAVEN, an agentic memory system for long-horizon robotic question answering and navigation. RAVEN stores visual embeddings with pose and time in a vector database, and grounds retrieval in a spatial map to answer queries and navigate to goals. By operating directly on visual embeddings, RAVEN avoids lossy image-to-text captioning and enables accurate semantic, spatial, and temporal retrieval at scale. Across several simulated and real-world video question-answering benchmarks, RAVEN consistently surpasses caption-based memory systems and matches frontier VLMs on long-horizon tasks at 10$\times$ lower retrieval cost. Finally, we instantiate RAVEN on a Unitree Go1 robot for the task of long-horizon navigation for natural language goal-reaching, and show successful deployment over several large indoor environments.

</details>


### [108] [Wear-Clearance-Impact Coupling in the Jansen Linkage: A Gait-Durability-Optimized Design Slows Joint Loosening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25208)
*Jichao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文构建了含间隙铰链的Theo Jansen行走机构前向动力学模型，耦合了Lankarani-Flores接触力、Ambrosio摩擦和Archard磨损定律，发现忽略间隙会低估峰值载荷约2倍，磨损集中在~10°载荷弧上（非均匀性导致局部间隙增长被低估~36倍），优化设计在统计上仍保持耐久性优势（磨损低9-7倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究将Jansen连杆机构引入耐久性设计并发现经典"圣数"被帕累托占优，但其将旋转铰链建模为理想无间隙销轴，磨损数据仅为相对排序而非服役退化预测。本文旨在放松这一理想化假设，建立含间隙的耦合动力学模型来预测真实磨损。

Method: 构建Jansen腿前向动力学模型：将旋转铰链变为含间隙铰链，采用Lankarani-Flores迟滞阻尼连续法向接触律和Ambrosio摩擦模型，集成为约束稳定的微分代数系统；耦合Archard磨损定律形成磨损→间隙→冲击反馈回路，进行16个随机相位的系综统计分析。

Result: (1) 忽略间隙低估峰值载荷：间隙模型接触力~104 N vs 理想~48 N（~2倍放大），两个间隙时升至~426 N；(2) 耦合对冲击高度敏感，单轨迹非单调可反转设计排序，但系综均值下优化设计鲁棒更耐久（单间隙磨损低9-7倍，双间隙低~1.7倍，p<0.01）；(3) 磨损集中在~10°载荷弧上，假设均匀间隙增长会低估局部增长~36倍。

Conclusion: 无间隙耐久性优势在混沌、多关节、非均匀磨损耦合的系综均值下仍得以保持。本文交付了首个含间隙耦合的前向动力学Jansen腿模型，并制定了可证伪的协议来测试各预测。

Abstract: A companion study introduced joint durability into the dimensional design of the Theo Jansen walking linkage and found its classical "holy numbers" Pareto-dominated, but it modelled the revolute joints as ideal, clearance-free pins, so its wear figures were relative rankings, not a prediction of in-service degradation. Here we relax that idealization. We build a forward-dynamic model of the Jansen leg in which a revolute joint becomes a clearance joint with a continuous normal contact law (Lankarani-Flores, hysteresis-damped) and Ambrosio friction, integrated as a constraint-stabilized differential-algebraic system, and couple it to the Archard law in a wear->clearance->impact feedback loop. Three findings emerge. First, neglecting clearance underestimates the peak joint load: the clearance model gives a peak contact force of ~104 N at the load-bearing pin against ~48 N for the ideal joint (~2x amplification), rising to ~426 N when two joints carry clearance at once. Second, the coupling is strongly impact-sensitive--single trajectories are non-monotonic and can reverse the design ranking, a chaos consistent with the literature--so designs must be compared statistically; over an ensemble of 16 randomized phases the optimized joint is robustly more durable, with per-cycle wear ~9-7x lower (peak force ~4x lower) at one clearance joint and still ~1.7x lower on both with two (p<0.01 throughout). Third, the wear is strongly non-uniform--it concentrates on a ~10 deg load arc--so assuming uniform clearance growth underestimates local clearance growth by ~36x. The clearance-free durability advantage thus survives the chaotic, multi-joint, non-uniformly-worn coupling in the ensemble mean. We deliver the first clearance-coupled forward-dynamic model of the Jansen leg and specify a falsifiable protocol to test each prediction.

</details>


### [109] [RigPI: Dynamic Parameter Identification of Rigid Body via VLM-Seeded Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25212)
*Xincheng He,Rongrong Zhang,Wei Jiang,Wenqiang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RigPI是一个系统框架，利用可微物理仿真、视觉语言模型先验和两阶段优化策略，从真实机器人交互中识别刚体和多连杆刚体的惯性及摩擦参数，在真实实验中对旋转/棱柱关节物体实现了精确稳定的参数估计和轨迹复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从真实世界交互中获取物理一致的惯性/摩擦参数面临感知噪声、建模误差和先验知识有限的挑战，现有方法难以实现高精度且物理合理的参数识别。

Method: 集成视觉语义先验(VLM提供初始化与约束搜索空间)、力/力矩测量和运动观测到可微仿真管线中，利用可微物理仿真器的梯度信息进行参数优化，采用两阶段优化策略降低噪声敏感性并避免物理不可行解。

Result: 在真实世界的旋转关节和棱柱关节物体上实现了精确稳定的参数估计，并在真实机器人上成功复现了操作轨迹，验证了参数感知的预测有效性。

Conclusion: RigPI框架在真实机器人系统辨识任务中展现了有效性和鲁棒性，为数字孪生和高级机器人操作中的物理参数识别提供了系统化解决方案。
